# Iteration 1

- strategy: `iteration_1.py`
- cost: $0.7877

## Rationale

The generator separates two concerns that were previously entangled: (1) producing syntactically well-formed, fast-to-parse TOML documents (the `normal_document` path), and (2) stressing the confirmed unbounded-recursion stack-overflow bug (the `deep_stress_document` path, isolated to a single `a = <deeply nested value>` line with nothing else in the document). Every recursive grammar rule (array_, inline_table) is expressed through `st.recursive`/`@composite` so nesting depth genuinely varies rather than being flattened, and is wrapped in `production(...)`; every terminal (string, integer, floating_point, bool_, date_time, dotted_key, table, array_table, key_value) calls `record_production`. Top-level key/table names are drawn from a uniqueness-guaranteed pool per document, removing an entire class of unintentional duplicate-key/table semantic rejects, while a small, deliberate, low-frequency branch still injects genuine duplicate-key/table/array-table-redefinition cases to exercise tomlc99's real rejection and superset-acceptance behavior. Known superset-acceptance edges (leading-zero integers, dot-less floats, raw control bytes in basic strings, minus the risky NUL byte) are retained at moderate frequency since they are confirmed accepts, not rejects. Deep-nesting stress documents sample depth broadly across 500-35000 (including the empirically bisected 23,000-27,000 crash-boundary band) for arrays, inline tables, and alternating array/inline-table mixes, each fully instrumented with per-level `production` calls so the depth histogram reflects genuine recursion.

## Changes from the previous iteration

- acceptance_rate=6.2% was diagnosed as most likely driven by ~12% of key_value values in EVERY ordinary document being an uncapped-depth (up to 30000) nested array/inline-table draw, meaning ~40% of documents contained a potentially slow/hanging deep parse that could drag the whole multi-line document to reject/timeout even when every other line was fine; fixed by moving all deep nesting into its own isolated single-line `deep_stress_document`, which can never contaminate an otherwise-valid document.
- Reduced normal-path recursion breadth (array items 5->3, inline-table pairs 4->3, max_leaves 20->10) so the bulk (80%) of documents generated via `normal_document` are fast, shallow-ish, and overwhelmingly well-formed, directly targeting the 'fix correctness first' guidance for the low 6.2% acceptance rate.
- Added a guaranteed-unique top-level key/table name pool per `normal_document` (via `st.lists(..., unique=True)`) to eliminate accidental cross-line key/table namespace collisions, which are real, spec-mandated tomlc99 rejects (duplicate key/table) that could previously occur unintentionally given ~7-8 random lines per document.
- Made inline_table_container use guaranteed-unique auto-generated keys (`k0`, `k1`, ...) instead of fully random keys, removing the (small but real) chance of accidental duplicate keys within a single inline table causing a spurious reject.
- Removed NUL (`\x00`) from CONTROL_BYTES: an uncertain, unconfirmed edge (the adaptations doc only measured 0x01) that could plausibly interact badly with C string handling and cause an unrelated reject; kept the other confirmed-accepted control bytes (0x01, 0x02, 0x1f, 0x7f).
- Retained and strengthened the depth-stress path per the prior run's histogram/no-crash finding: depth now sampled with explicit mass in both 500-15000 and 15000-35000 so the confirmed flaky/deterministic crash boundaries (~23,793 for arrays, ~26,167 for inline tables) are hit more often, plus a new `deep_mixed_value` alternating array/inline-table nesting to probe recursion paths that alternate between the two container kinds, per the 'depth is the primary lever' guidance.
- Slightly de-emphasized numeric edge-case density (leading-zero integer, dot-less float now one option among several rather than uniformly 1-in-5/1-in-6) per the target-specific advisory that tomlc99's numeric handling is comprehensive and low-payoff, freeing weight toward the string/byte-level and depth axes the notes flag as higher value.
- Kept the duplicate-key/table/array-table-redefinition low-frequency injection (now ~5% via extra_choice bucket of 60) using names outside the unique pool, preserving deliberate coverage of the documented subset-rejection and superset-acceptance semantic gaps without contributing to accidental collisions elsewhere.
